# Theory of Finite Quantum Mechanics: Master Derivation Index

This directory contains **one standalone Markdown paper per derivation step** tracking finite constructions, source records, explicit assumptions and open obligations from the inert inscription `<>` through the finite quantum programme and its application branches.

Every paper declares its parent prerequisites, mathematical statement, exact rational / Lean 4 witnesses, and downstream consequences.

---

## Application and composition derivation branches

These branches attach to existing finite carriers. A proved theorem has its stated hypotheses; an open research target remains open. See the [branch fragment](../fragments/application_branches.json) and the [initial application receipt](../research/application_branches_validation.json). The [tensor receipt](../research/tensor_composition_validation.json) is the historical prose/kernel snapshot; the [formalization receipt](../research/tensor_formalization_validation.json) records the verified tomography and Schmidt extension. The [measurement receipt](../research/tensor_measurement_validation.json) records the effect cone, binary interval and finite POVMs with explicit saturation premises. The [countermodel receipt](../research/tensor_countermodel_validation.json) verifies the TFQ-0413 hypothesis boundaries.

## Frontier derivation branches

These branches record external programmes and their candidate finite targets. A source record or an imported assumption is not a result of this repository; an open target stays open until a run checks it. A gap route marks a link that no source read for this change derives. See the [branch fragment](../fragments/frontier_branches.json).

## Genesis to higher categories

This stage records open steps and named imports from the Genesis admission gate toward finite shadows, truncation, horn fillers, a finite identity-type model, three infinite imports, and a priced-import language. An imported assumption is not a result of this repository. A gap route marks a link that no source derives. The limit node states Gödel-Rosser and Tarski under hypotheses that do not mention the unfinished language; its route is reading order. See the [branch fragment](../fragments/genesis_to_higher.json).

## FOC-035 observer presheaf

This stage maps the claim ledger of FOC-035 to nodes at the status the evidence supports in this repository. PROVED_LEAN rows keep the narrowed statements of `lean4/LggrFiniteResults.lean`; the fixed-ceiling campaign example retains CONDITIONAL despite its scoped Lean references. Rows computed by a Rust test stay conditional, because that test needs an external crate and is not run here. Instance values are recomputed by an exact independent script, and the Python gate and one repaired test run here. Cited results are imported assumptions. A gap route marks a link that no source derives. See the [branch fragment](../fragments/foc035_observer_presheaf.json).
